# The Science of House Dimensions

<margin>(143)</margin>
If the count is twenty-two, even enemies will tremble in fear;
Great joy shall arrive, and prosperity will abound.
The children born—both sons and daughters—will achieve great fame.
The Lord <note>Shiva</note> spoke this science with singular focus to the Goddess <note>Parvati; original: "Umaiyaval"</note>.

<margin>(144)</margin>
If the count is twenty-three, every kind of disease will manifest;
Whatever comes will quickly depart, and life’s comforts will be meager.
The effects of past deeds <term>Karma (Iruvinai)</term> will be heavy, and those living there will face ruin.
They may be forced to leave their land, and even the rains will fail them.

<margin>(145)</margin>
If the base is twenty-six, life will be short and vitality will diminish.
If it is twenty-one, the grace of the Goddess will be lost.
But if it is twenty-eight, they shall receive the wealth of the Goddess of Fortune <note>Indira/Lakshmi</note>,
And live in perfect unity—so the Lord told the Goddess.

<margin>(146)</margin>
If it is twenty-seven, behold the rising of great fortune!
They will live a life without decline, respected by everyone in the world.
Even barren lands will yield fruit and become productive;
No ancient bad karma shall affect them—see what the Supreme Lord has said.

<margin>(147)</margin>
If it is twenty-eight, the enemy will stand in fear;
The body will be healthy, and blessings will be limitless.
Nothing shall be lost, friend, by the grace of the Lion-like Lord <note>Kesari/Vishnu or Shiva</note>;
No obstacles shall cross their path—so said the Lord of the Cosmic Dance <note>Thandava Moorthi/Shiva</note>.

<margin>148</margin>
If it is twenty-nine, wealth will rise up at that moment.
If it is thirty <note>original: "ten and twenty"</note>, I tell you the truth:
Many fortunes will arrive without any exhaustion or fatigue;
Sons and daughters will be gained—so the Lord of the Madmen <note>a title for Shiva</note> declared.

<margin>(149)</margin>
If it is thirty-one, the grace of the Three-Eyed Lord <note>Shiva</note> will be present.
If it is thirty-two, by the grace of the Protector <note>Mukundan/Vishnu</note>,
The possessions stored and the children born will flourish across the whole world.
I speak no lie; this is what the Destroyer <note>Sankara/Shiva</note> told the Goddess.

<margin>(150)</margin>
If it is thirty-three <note>original: "five-sixes and three"</note>, the family residing there will be good.
If it is thirty-four, it will drive the residing family away.
If it is thirty-five, the Eightfold Lakshmi <term>Ashta-Lakshmi <note>The eight forms of the Goddess of Wealth</note></term> will join them.
If it is thirty-six, they shall rule as kings among kings.

<margin>(151)</margin>
If it is thirty-seven, there will be a life of sweet joy.
If it is thirty-eight, demons <note>ghosts/misfortune</note> will always frequent the place.
If it is thirty-nine, there will be sweetness and physical comfort.
If it is forty, there will always be a sense of ease and clarity.

<margin>(152)</margin>
If it is forty-one, there will be the delightful wealth of the God of Riches <note>Kubera</note>.
If it is forty-seven, the Goddess Lakshmi will take up residence there.
But surely, if it is forty-five, there is no glory; fire will break out.
And mark this: if it is forty-eight, their very eyes will lose their sight.

->To know the circumference and length for the depth of a well<-

For a 4-foot depth, the length is 10 feet; for a 5-foot depth, the length is 15 feet; for a 6-foot depth, the length is 18 feet; for a 7-foot depth, the length is 21 feet; for an 8-foot depth, the length is 24 feet.

### Auspicious Timing for Digging a Well

In the month of *Aippasi* <note>October-November</note>, on the 30th day, the following stars are auspicious: *Uttiram*, *Anusham*, *Kettai*, *Revati*, *Chithirai*, *Rohini*, *Thiruvonam*, *Hastam*, *Mrigashirsha*, *Pooradam*, and *Magam*. The 5th, 7th, 3rd, 11th, 13th, and 1st lunar days are good. The zodiac signs of Aquarius, Pisces, Capricorn, Taurus, and Cancer, along with auspicious weekdays, are excellent. In the month of *Chithirai* <note>April-May</note>, on the 30th day, as mentioned before, these eighteen factors combined are excellent for beginning the work.

## The Position of the Arugakan
<note>The "Arugakan" likely refers to a directional deity or a symbolic force, possibly Argha or a specific architectural spirit, whose location changes based on the time and day.</note>

On Saturday night for 30 <term>Nazhigai</term> <note>A unit of time equal to 24 minutes</note> and Sunday daytime for 30 Nazhigai, he is in the South. On Sunday night for 20... 30 Nazhigai, he is in the Southwest. On Tuesday daytime for 30 and at night for 10, he is in the Northwest. On Wednesday daytime for 30, he is in the Northwest. On Wednesday night for 3, and Thursday daytime for 40 and at night for 10, he is in the North. For the remaining 20, and on Friday for 60, he is in the North. On Saturday daytime for 30, he is in the East. The Arugakan resides in the Southeast <note>Agni corner</note>.

---

## The Positions of the Directional Animals
<note>original: "Dishayoni Nilai"</note>

| | | | |
|---|---|---|---|
| Garuda (Eagle) | East | Cat | Southeast |
| Lion | South | Dog | Southwest |
| Snake | West | Rat | Northwest |
| Elephant | North | Rabbit | Northeast |

When looking at the positions starting from the Garuda of the Arugakan, the enemy <term>Yoni</term> <note>In this context, "Yoni" refers to the symbolic animal nature or "womb" of a direction</note> will stand opposite. Among these, one may travel from the direction of the Garuda to the direction of the Snake; however, one must not travel from the direction of the Snake to the direction of the Garuda. This is because the Snake will become the prey that the Garuda goes to take. Therefore, when a person observes omens such as birds before a journey, these directional animal rules must be considered.

## Verse

<insert>
In the position of the Arugakan, the Garuda and a Cat,
Standing close together, and the Dog that appears.
</insert>

## Purity of the Lunar Days <note>original: "Mada Suddham" - refers to the auspiciousness of the Tithi or lunar day</note>

To enter a new house: 12th day; For a Coronation: 11th day.
For a child's first solid food: 10th day; For tonsure: 1st day.
For Upanayana <note>sacred thread ceremony</note>: 8th day; For Marriage: 7th day.
For wearing new jewelry: 9th day; For Travel: 9th day.
For beginning education: 4th day; For wearing gold: 2nd day.
For Initiation <note>Diksha</note>: the signs and planetary positions must be perfectly pure.

## Special Significance of the Weekdays

Sunday is best for starting a job; Monday for sowing seeds; Tuesday for engaging in battle; Wednesday for beginning education; Thursday for performing a marriage; Friday for tonsure; Saturday for performing penance.

## The Flaw of Vishti <note>Vishti Karana - an inauspicious period in the lunar calendar</note>

In the waxing phase <note>Shukla Paksha</note>: on the 4th day, Vishti resides in the 5th watch <note>Yama - a period of 3 hours</note> in the West; on the 8th day, in the 2nd watch in the Southeast; on the 11th day, in the 7th watch in the North; on the Full Moon day, in the 4th watch in the Southwest. In the waning phase <note>Krishna Paksha</note>: on the 3rd day, in the 6th watch in the Northwest; on the 7th day, in the 9th watch in the South; on the 10th day, in the 8th watch in the Northeast; on the 14th day, in the 3rd watch in the East. Because Vishti "consumes" these times, one should not perform auspicious deeds or undertake travel during these specific watches.

<sig>Su. Ni.</sig>

> On the fourth of the waxing moon, the fifth watch,
> On the eighth and the full moon, the eighth and first,
> On the seventh, the fourth watch of the day,
> In the waning half, on the third, the sixth watch,
> On the seventh, the ninth watch,
> On the tenth, the eighth watch,
> On the eleventh, the first watch—these are called Vishti.
> Knowing these watches from Sunday onwards,
> One should act according to the prescribed order.

# The Faults of the Five Planets

The 7th star <note>original: nakshatra; the lunar mansions used in Indian astrology</note> from the star where the Sun resides is called *Utpata* <note>calamity</note>; the 10th star is called *Naraka* <note>hell</note>; the 18th star <note>Karma nakshatra</note> is called *Brahmadanda* <note>the staff of Brahma/punishment</note>; and the 21st star is called *Kalan* <note>death/time</note>.

From the star where **Mars** resides, the 7th star is called *Shula* <note>the trident/piercing</note>; the 10th star is called *Sakara*; the 18th star is called *Akhanda*; and the 21st star is called *Kalan*.

From the star where **Mercury** resides, the 8th, 10th, 18th, and 26th stars are called *Akhanda*.

From the star where **Venus** resides, the 7th and 10th stars are considered severe. From the star where **Jupiter** resides, the 7th and 10th stars are also called severe.

From the star where **Saturn** resides, the 5th, 6th, 8th, 11th, and 20th stars are called *Bandhaka* <note>binding/imprisoning</note>.

From the stars where **Rahu** and **Ketu** <note>the north and south lunar nodes</note> reside, the 7th star is called *Parigha* <note>an iron club</note>. These are all unsuitable for performing auspicious deeds.

-> Dates on which "Kari Naal" <note>Black Days/Inauspicious days</note> occur in the months <-

| | |
|---|---|
| Chithirai (Apr-May) - 6, 15 | Karthigai (Nov-Dec) - 1, 10, 17 |
| Vaikasi (May-Jun) - 7, 16, 17 | Margazhi (Dec-Jan) - 1, 6, 11 |
| Aani (Jun-Jul) - 1, 6 | Thai (Jan-Feb) - 1, 2, 3, 11, 17 |
| Aadi (Jul-Aug) - 2, 10, 20 | Masi (Feb-Mar) - 15, 16, 17 |
| Avani (Aug-Sep) - 2, 9, 28 | Panguni (Mar-Apr) - 6, 15, 16 |
| Purattasi (Sep-Oct) - 16, 29 | |
| Aippasi (Oct-Nov) - 6, 15, 20, 27, 28 | |

> In the joyful month of Aries <note>Chithirai</note>, the 6th and 15th;
> In the month of the Bull <note>Vaikasi</note>, the 6th, 16th, and 17th;
> In the month of great love, Gemini <note>Aani</note>, the 1st and 6th; in Cancer <note>Aadi</note>,
> The 2nd, 10th, and 20th; in Leo <note>Avani</note>, the 2nd,
> 9th, and 28th; in Virgo <note>Purattasi</note>, the 16th and 29th;
> In Libra <note>Aippasi</note>, the 6th; in Scorpio <note>Karthigai</note>,
> The 1st, 10th, and 17th;
> In Sagittarius <note>Margazhi</note>, the 1st, 6th, and 11th are the days of the Sage;
> In the month of Capricorn <note>Thai</note>, the 1st, 2nd,
> 10th, 11th, and 17th are unsuitable;

> And 17th; in the final month of Pisces <note>Panguni</note>, the 6th,
> 15th, and 16th are greatly evil.
> Thus, for every month mentioned, take these as the "Kari" days;
> These thirty-four days were declared by the Sage to be avoided,
> Know these as the "Kari" days that hinder progress.

### Ascendant-Tyajyam
<note>Lagnas or zodiac signs to be avoided at specific intervals.</note>

For Aries, Taurus, Virgo, and Sagittarius—a total of 4—the first 10 *nazhigai* are avoided. For Gemini, Leo, Libra, and Aquarius—a total of 4—the middle 10 *nazhigai* are avoided. For Cancer, Scorpio, Capricorn, and Pisces—a total of 4—the final 10 *nazhigai* are avoided.

### Fixed Signs
<note>Sthirarasi: Signs associated with stability and permanence, usually preferred for foundation laying.</note>

Taurus, Scorpio, Aquarius, and Leo—totaling 4.

## Dual Signs <note>original: "Upaya Rasi" - refers to zodiac signs that possess a dual nature, neither purely fixed nor purely movable.</note>

->Aries, Gemini, Leo, Libra, Sagittarius, and Aquarius<-

### Verse on Rahu Kaalam <note>Rahu Kaalam is an inauspicious period of the day associated with the shadow planet Rahu.</note>

> For the Sun (Sunday) it is twenty-six and a quarter <note>The units refer to Nazhigai, a traditional unit of time where 1 Nazhigai equals 24 minutes.</note>
> For the Moon (Monday) it is three and three-quarters
> For the Fire-colored one (Tuesday) it is twenty-two and a half
> For Mercury (Wednesday) it is fifteen
> For Jupiter (Thursday) it is eighteen and three-quarters
> For the Cool Venus (Friday) it is eleven and a quarter
> For the Dark One (Saturday) it is seven and a half—thus know the times of Rahu.

### Weekly Gulika <note>Gulika is a mathematical point in the sky, considered the son of Saturn; its timing is crucial for determining auspicious moments.</note>

| Day | | Night | |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Sunday | 22.5 | Sunday | 11.25 |
| Monday | 18.75 | Monday | 7.5 |
| Tuesday | 15 | Tuesday | 3.75 |
| Wednesday | 11.25 | Wednesday | 26.25 |
| Thursday | 7.5 | Thursday | 22.5 |
| Friday | 3.75 | Friday | 18.75 |
| Saturday | 26.25 | Saturday | 15 |

### Daytime Gulika

> For the Sun, the seventh Muhurtha <note>A Muhurtha is a traditional unit of time lasting approximately 48 minutes.</note>
> For the good Moon, the sixth; for Mars, the fifth;
> For Mercury, the fourth; for the golden Jupiter, the third;
> For the pure Venus, the second; and for Saturn, the first.
> Calculate the Gulika according to these morning Muhurthas.

### Nighttime Gulika

> For the Sun, it is the third; for the beautiful Moon, the second;
> For the warring Mars, the first; for the stated Mercury, the seventh;
> For the wisdom-increasing Jupiter, the sixth; for the respected Venus, the fifth;
> For Saturn, the fourth; take these night Muhurthas as you did for the day.

### Planetary Aspects <note>original: "Graha Parvai" - the influence or "gaze" a planet casts upon other zodiac houses from its current position.</note>

> Jupiter looks at the 5th, 7th, and 9th houses.
> Saturn looks at the 3rd, 7th, and 10th houses.
> Mars looks at the 4th, 7th, and 8th houses.
> Gulika looks at the 7th and 12th houses.
> All other planets look at the 7th house.

### Strength of the Aspect

> 1st and 7th: Full Aspect (100% influence)
> 8th and 9th: Three-Quarter Aspect (75% influence)
> 5th: Half Aspect (50% influence)
> 4th: Quarter Aspect (25% influence)
> 3rd and 11th: One-Eighth Aspect (12.5% influence)

## Verse

## Housewarming <note>original: "Grihapravesam"</note>

The months of June-July <note>Ani</note>, July-August <note>Adi</note>, September-October <note>Purattasi</note>, December-January <note>Margazhi</note>, February-March <note>Masi</note>, and March-April <note>Panguni</note>—along with Sundays, Tuesdays, and Thursdays—and the stars Ashwini, Mrigashirsha, Punarvasu, Hasta, Chitra, and Swati (these three stars), Anuradha, and Shravana—excluding other stars—and the signs of Scorpio and Aquarius are not auspicious. An Ascendant <term>Lagna: the degree of the zodiac rising on the eastern horizon</term> that is pure in the eighth house is best.

## Night-Waking Zodiac Signs

Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Sagittarius, Capricorn—Total: 6.

## Impurities in the Earth <note>original: "Bhumi Salliyam"</note>—Another Method

> If Saturn stands with the bones of the earth in the house of the spouse,
> And if one dwells in that house, the wife shall meet her death.
> Great diseases will arise, along with doubt and distress;
> It will bring ruin to the flourishing lineages—so said the Creator <note>Mayan, the divine architect</note>.

<margin>(153)</margin>
(**Meaning**) If planets stand in the seventh house from the Rising Sign <note>Udaya Varuda Rasi</note>, and if the Moon stands in the first, fourth, seventh, or tenth positions from them, it is certain that there are impurities <term>Salliyam: hidden objects like bones or charcoal buried in the ground that cause bad luck</term> in the soil. The result: it will kill the women, bring ruin to the lineage, and cause loss of wealth and life. There is no happiness in that house.

> If charcoal and bones are joined together in the house,
> Great laziness will prevail, and you shall see no comfort there.
> There will be fear of snakes and enmity with the wife;
> Thus it is categorized across the world by looking at many great books.

<margin>(154)</margin>
(**Meaning**) If planets stand in the Rising Signs and the Moon stands in the angular positions <term>Kendras: the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses</term> to them, one must know that there are ashes and bones in that house. The result: there will be laziness and fear of snakes. One must say there will be enmity with the wife, wealth will perish, and there will be constant doubt.

> If impurities are found in the house of children,
> Such as stones, sturdy wood, or logs,
> Or if bones are gathered there and one dwells in that great house,
> The lineages will be severed—so said the Father, Mayan.

(**Meaning**) If a group of planets stands in the fifth house from the Rising Sign, and the Moon stands in the angular positions to them, then black stones, wood, and logs [are present]...

<sig>6</sig>

# The Science of Architecture

If it is said that bones are present, the results will be as follows: they will lead to destruction. (155)

> If hidden defects are located in the second house from the "Life" sign,
> And if the Moon joins that point, it is not auspicious for the masters;
> Courage, strength, and wealth will all be destroyed—
> So said the sage Mayan <note>The divine architect and author of the Surya Siddhanta</note> to the lady with the voice of a cuckoo.

(Meaning) "Life" refers to the *Arudha Lagna* <note>The rising sign at the time of inquiry</note> and the *Chandra Lagna* <note>The sign where the Moon is positioned</note>. If the Moon stands in the second house from these, it should be known as *Shallyam* <note>term: Shallyam; hidden impurities or foreign objects like bones or charcoal buried underground that cause bad luck</note>. The result is that it will not be good for the owner of that house; courage, strength, and wealth will all perish. (156)

> If black stone and bones are found in the house of action,
> Or if the nodes or malefic planets join in the final degrees,
> Precious wealth will be ruined and the house itself will fall—
> So said the sage Mayan to the lady of the beautiful world.

(Meaning) If planets stand in the tenth house from the "House of Action" <note>The Karma Sthana, relating to one's deeds and effects</note>, then black stones, bones, or other impurities will be present. The result is that wealth will be lost, people will suffer harm, the house will fall into ruin, and terrifying dreams will occur. (157)

> If hidden defects are found in the sixth, eighth, or twelfth houses,
> And they are positioned there, there is no fault, only good;
> But if they stand in the seventh, the profitable eleventh, or the third,
> The named defects will cause great and chronic diseases.

(Meaning) If planets stand in the sixth, eighth, or twelfth houses <note>Traditionally considered "dusthana" or difficult houses, where the presence of a defect's indicator may actually neutralize the harm</note>, it results in good. If planets stand in the seventh or third houses, there is a *Shallyam* defect; it will cause "Karmic disease" <note>term: Kanma-viyathi; a chronic illness believed to be the result of past life actions</note>. (158)

> If jars and bones are found together in the flourishing house,
> It is like a swift ship breaking apart in the middle of the ocean;
> If they remain in the joined house, they will destroy the lineage—
> Thus the Creator <note>original: "Ayan" (Brahma/Mayan)</note> sang and spoke, knowing the results.

(Meaning) To determine if jars and bones are present: if planets stand in the *Udaya Lagna* <note>The ascending sign</note> and the Moon is in the quadrants <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>, know that there is a defect. The result is like a speeding ship breaking in the ocean; the good fortune will vanish, the women of the house may face death, and the entire lineage will suffer harm. (159)

> If Rahu stands in the Arudha, and on the seventh, fourth, or tenth days,
> Saturn and the Moon are positioned, then charcoal and bones
> Will be found with certainty; furthermore, if in the second or third,
> The Sun and Moon stand, there will also be iron.

(Meaning) If Rahu <note>The shadow planet representing the north node of the moon</note> stands in the *Arudha* signs at the time of rising, or if Saturn and the Moon stand in the seventh, fourth, or tenth positions, then charcoal and bones are present...

<summary>This page details astrological calculations used in the Shilpa Shastra tradition to detect "Shallyam" (hidden underground impurities like bones or charcoal) that can cause illness or ruin for a homeowner. It cites the sage Mayan as the authority on how planetary positions in specific houses determine the presence and consequences of these hidden defects.</summary>

Firmly in the fourth, seventh, or tenth houses, with the Moon;
Broken pots, wood, bones, and ash,
The sage with matted hair <note>likely referring to Sage Agastya</note> said these will be found.

(Meaning) When someone comes to inquire, ask them to touch a sign on the zodiac circle. If Saturn, Sun, Mars, or Rahu are in the fourth, seventh, or tenth houses from that sign, and if the waning Moon is also present, you must say that there are dead trees, potsherds, bones, and ash buried there.

And if the Sun is in the eighth or fourth house before that,

He surely said that there will be the benefit of hidden treasure.

(Meaning) If Mercury or other planets are in the fourth, seventh, or tenth house from the Moon, and the Sun is also present, you must say that there is a hidden treasure <note>original: "nidhi puthaiyal"</note> in that spot. <sig>33.</sig>

If they stand without retrograde motion, the King's business will succeed;
Without doubt, wealth will be found within the earth,
There will be much silver found in that ground.

(Meaning) If Venus and Mercury are together and the Sun is opposite them, and if they are not in retrograde, and if Saturn is in the fourth house from the Sun, you must say there is wealth in that land. If not wealth, at least silver will be there. <meta> catchword: Houses </meta>

# 48
## The Science of Architecture

In the month of Karthigai <note>mid-November to mid-December</note>, for a newly built house, the entrance doorframe may be placed facing South.
In the month of Maasi <note>mid-February to mid-March</note>, for a newly built house, the entrance doorframe may be placed facing West.
In the month of Vaikasi <note>mid-May to mid-June</note>, for a newly built house, the entrance doorframe may be placed facing West.

---

->In whichever month the doorframe is set, it must be placed according to the knowledge of the directions.<-

If a house is built when the Sun resides in the two zodiac signs of Cancer or Leo, the entrance doorframe may be placed facing East.

If a house is built when the Sun resides in the two zodiac signs of Libra or Scorpio, the entrance doorframe may be placed facing South.

If a house is built when the Sun resides in the two zodiac signs of Capricorn or Aquarius, the entrance doorframe may be placed facing West.

If a house is built when the Sun resides in the signs of Virgo, Gemini, Sagittarius, or Pisces, the doorframe should not be set. <note>These are considered 'dual' or 'mutable' signs, often deemed unstable for such foundational work in Vedic astrology.</note>

For those born under other signs: for those of Scorpio, Pisces, and Cancer, the house should face East.

For those of Taurus and Capricorn, the house should face South.

For those of Libra, Gemini, and Aquarius, the house should face West.

For those of Sagittarius, Aries, and Leo, the house should face North.

It is good if the direction of the house is friendly <note>astrologically compatible</note> to one's own birth sign.

->The Rule for Placing the Doorframe According to the Directions<-

For houses with an East-facing entrance doorframe, measure the distance from the Northeast corner <term>Ishanya: the corner of the gods</term> to the Southeast corner <term>Agni-moolai: the corner of fire</term>. Divide this into 8 parts. The first part is "Ali" <note>meaning 'sterile' or 'unproductive'</note>.

| Part | Result |
|-------|------|
| 2nd Part | Theft |
| 3rd Part | Attainment of Wealth |
| 4th Part | Royal Honor |
| 5th Part | Increase of Children |
| 6th Part | Happiness |
| 7th Part | Sorrow |
| 8th Part | Disease |

---

For a West-facing entrance doorframe, measure from the Southwest corner <term>Niruti-moolai: the corner of the ancestors/demons</term> to the Northwest corner <term>Vayu-moolai: the corner of the wind</term> and divide into 8 parts...

->Determining the placement of the door frame<-
->by knowing the influences of the nine planets<-

For an east-facing doorway, measure the distance from the Northeast to the Southeast corner and divide it into 9 parts.

For a south-facing doorway, measure the distance from the Southeast to the Southwest corner and divide it into 9 parts.

For a west-facing doorway, divide the distance from the Southwest corner to the Northwest corner into nine parts.

<note>The text transitions from an 8-part division system to a 9-part system based on the Navagrahas (nine planetary deities).</note>

<page-num>44</page-num>
<header>The Science of Sculpture (Shilpa Shastra),</header>

For a doorway facing North, divide the space from the Northwest corner <note>Vayu corner</note> to the Northeast corner <note>Ishanya corner</note> into 9 parts.

In these four directions, the parts are assigned as follows: 1st part—Sun; 2nd part—Moon; 3rd part—Mars; 4th part—Mercury; 5th part—Jupiter; 6th part—Venus; 7th part—Saturn; 8th part—Rahu; 9th part—Ketu. Among these nine planetary divisions <note>Navagrahas</note>, the main temple doorway should be placed in the division belonging to Jupiter <note>Brihaspati</note>.

The doorway for a house may be placed in the division of Venus.
The doorway for a cattle shed may be placed in the division of Saturn.

Furthermore, the divisions of Mercury, Jupiter, and Venus are all suitable for placing the doorways of temples, houses, and cattle sheds.

The door should be hung on the left side of the doorframe.
A doorframe measuring seven spans <term>shaan</term> <note>a traditional unit of measurement roughly equal to the span of a hand</note> in length and 3 spans in width is excellent.
A doorframe of 6 spans in length and 3 spans in width is also excellent.
A doorframe should not be made from wood that has been damaged or scarred; likewise, one should not use three different types of wood in the construction of a single building.

The door itself must be constructed using an odd number of wooden planks; it should not be made by joining an even number of planks.

When entering the house, there should be no beam pointing directly at the entrance <note>thoola-kuthu: a structural flaw believed to direct negative energy</note>.
If there is a shop wall directly opposite the house entrance, it will result in fear of enemies.

The street level should not be higher than your house's threshold; if the house threshold is lower than the street, the prosperity coming to the home will vanish.

### Auspicious Timing (Muhurta)

->If temples, cities, towns, long-lasting tanks, or wells are made,
And if the work usually performed by a virtuous artisan <term>shilpan</term> is instead
Performed by a person of another caste from elsewhere on this earth,
It will bring great distress and defects to the kings and the patron alike.<-

(Meaning): If temples, cities, towns, water tanks, or wells are not constructed by the hands of a traditional artisan <note>shilpan</note>, but the inaugural rites <term>muhurta</term> are performed by outsiders, it will bring ill fortune to both the ruler and the owner.

### The Measuring Rod

->Eight tiny specks of dust are equal to one particle of cotton;
Eight particles of cotton are equal to the tip of a fine hair;
Eight hair-tips are equal to one grain of sand;
And thirty-two <note>four times eight</note> grains of sand, it is said, make one unit.<-

(Meaning) Eight atoms <note>original: "Anu"</note> make one speck of dust. Eight specks of dust make one hair-tip. Eight hair-tips make one grain of sand. Eight grains of sand make one mustard seed. Eight mustard seeds make one grain of bamboo rice. (166)

> If eight bamboo seeds are joined, it becomes one finger-width <term>Angulam: a unit of measurement roughly equal to an inch</term>.
> If that finger-width is taken six times, it is the lowest of the low.
> If seven times two <note>meaning 14</note> it is considered the low.
> If three-quarters <note>of a full cubit</note> are seen, it is considered the medium.

(Meaning) Eight bamboo seeds make one finger-width. Six finger-widths make a quarter-cubit <note>original: "kaal-muzham"</note>; this is named the "lowest of the low" <note>original: "adhama-adhamam"</note>. Twelve finger-widths make a half-cubit; this is named the "low" <note>original: "adhamam"</note>. Eighteen finger-widths make a three-quarter cubit; this is named the "medium" <note>original: "madhyamam"</note>. (167)

> If it becomes one full unit, that cubit is the supreme.
> For the Brahmins, the excellent Kshatriyas, Shudras,
> and the wise Vaishyas, to receive prosperity,
> I have composed these verses according to the established justice.

(Meaning) Twenty-four finger-widths make one cubit <term>Muzham: the distance from the elbow to the tip of the middle finger</term>. This is named the "supreme" <note>original: "uttamam"</note> and the "human scale." Therefore, we shall describe which cubit-measure applies to the Brahmins, Kshatriyas, Vaishyas, and Shudras. (168)

### The Five Periods of Vastu and their Characteristics — Different Meter

When the Vastu deity wakes and gazes with steady eyes,
Within the duration of three and three-quarter <term>Ghadikas</term><note>A traditional unit of time; 1 Ghadika equals 24 minutes</note>,
The first of the two periods is three-quarters of a Ghadika;
If the King performs teeth-cleaning then, fear shall arise.

(Meaning) Regarding the characteristics of the Vastu deity's authority: within the three and three-quarter Ghadikas, the first period lasts three-quarters of a Ghadika. During this time, the deity performs teeth-cleaning. If a Muhurta is performed then, there will be fear of the government or the King. (611)

In the second period that follows, for three-quarters of a Ghadika,
He performs his bath; if done then, suffering will occur.
In the third period mentioned, for three-quarters of a Ghadika,
He performs his worship; those who act then will wither in poverty.

(Meaning) The second period of Vastu lasts three-quarters of a Ghadika, during which he bathes. If a Muhurta is performed then, it leads to affliction. The third period lasts three-quarters of a Ghadika, during which he performs worship. If a Muhurta is performed then, they will suffer from poverty. (612)

In the fourth period, for three-quarters of a Ghadika,
He takes his precious meal; a beautiful son will be born.
In the fifth period of skill, for three-quarters of a Ghadika,
He receives the <term>Tambula</term><note>Betel leaf and areca nut, consumed after a meal</note>; declare this as great joy and excellence.

(Meaning) The fourth period consists of three-quarters of a Ghadika for eating. If a Muhurta is performed then, one will be blessed with children. The fifth period consists of three-quarters of a Ghadika for taking Tambula. If a Muhurta is performed then, it is highly auspicious. (613)

### Characteristics of the Taste and Smell of Soil on a Plot — Different Meter

Curd, ghee, honey, oil, and even blood,
The smell of fish, or other foul odors,
Even if crops are planted, they will not yield strength; the house will go to ruin,
These are the qualities of the soil within the forest of the diamond-spear-wielder.

(Meaning) Regarding the scent of the earth: if the ground smells like curd, ghee, honey, blood, hair, or raw fish, even if crops are planted, they will not grow. If a house is built on such land, it will fall into ruin.

And having observed the auspicious stars and successful days...

->The Wood Used for Making the Gnomon<-

For the supreme gnomon, the height shall be one cubit <term>Muzham: a traditional measurement from the elbow to the tip of the middle finger</term>.
Listen as I describe the base, which should be six inches <term>Angulam: a thumb's breadth or inch</term> wide.
The tip at the head should be exactly one inch.
With devotion, turn it beautifully and with care on a lathe.
**Meaning:** The method of making the gnomon is as follows: height of one cubit, thickness at the base of six inches, and thickness at the tip of one inch. It should be turned on a lathe to be perfectly round and smooth.

The Woods for the Gnomon According to the Four Classes

Sandalwood and ivory are fit for the Gods.
Wonderfully, for the Priests <note>Brahmins</note>, ebony and Vanni are prescribed.
For the Kings <note>Kshatriyas</note>, teak is suitable.
The heartwood of the tamarind is said to be for the Laborers <note>Sudras</note>.
**Meaning:** Ivory and sandalwood are for the Gods; ebony is for the Priests; teak is for the Kings; and tamarind heartwood is for the Laborers.

(Meaning) During the Northern Solstice <note>original: "Uttarayana"</note> and the Southern Solstice <note>original: "Dakshinayana"</note>, there are specific shadow measurements to be observed. By placing a cord between the two points marked within that circle, we shall determine the East and West directions. (180)

Upon the two points, within the clear workspace,

### Deviations of the Shadow

In the months of Vaikasi <note>May–June</note> and the great Purattasi <note>September–October</note>, 
There is no shadow deviation; listen as I explain this clearly. 
In the months of Avani <note>August–September</note>, Ani <note>June–July</note>, Aippasi <note>October–November</note>, and Chithirai <note>April–May</note>, 
The shadow deviation is said to be two inches <note>term: "Angulam" (a traditional unit of measurement approximately equal to a thumb's width)</note>.

(Meaning) There is no shadow correction required for the months of Vaikasi and Purattasi. For the four months of Avani, Ani, Aippasi, and Chithirai, the deviation is two inches. (183)

In Adi <note>July–August</note>, Karthigai <note>November–December</note>, and Panguni <note>March–April</note>, the shadow deviation is four inches. 
In the long months of Margazhi <note>December–January</note> and Masi <note>February–March</note>, the shadow is six fingers <note>inches</note> long. 
In the sought-after month of Thai <note>January–February</note>, the deviation is eight inches; 
I share this traditional wisdom so that your fame may be sung.

(Meaning) For the three months of Adi, Karthigai, and Panguni, the deviation is four inches. For Margazhi and Masi, the deviation is six inches. For the month of Thai, the deviation is eight inches. (184)

### Omens

A house lizard, water pots, a washerman, 
Brightly polished vessels, milk, cooked rice, meat, and pots of honey, 
A maiden, a courtesan, or a bundle of sugarcane—if these appear before you, 
Or if a pair of Brahmins <note>priests</note> approach from the opposite direction, the undertaking will be successful.

# Science of House Foundations
<note>original: "Manaiadi Sastram" — a traditional manual for architecture and domestic life</note>

(Meaning) Regarding omens: if a full water pot, a washerman, a pot of curd, a pig, a swan, a pot of liquor, a pregnant woman, a temple dancer <note>original: "Devadasi"</note>, a bundle of sugarcane, or two Brahmins <note>original: "parppar"</note> come toward you, it is said to be highly auspicious. <sig>(185)</sig>

> If the fox, the tiger, the hare, the rooster, the crane,
> The beautiful peacock, the garden lizard, the deer, or the cow,
> The fragrant heron, the squirrel, the donkey, or the crow-pheasant,
> Move toward the right side, the intended task will be successful.

(Meaning) If a fox, tiger, hare, rooster, crane, peacock, garden lizard, deer, cow, heron, squirrel, donkey, or crow-pheasant <note>original: "chemboothu"</note> crosses to the right side, it is very auspicious. <sig>(186)</sig>

> If these are encountered, it is like meeting Death himself.

(Meaning) If a person wearing beautiful clothes <note>The verse suggests specific red/gold tones associated with fire or anger</note>, a person with reddish hair, a person with a stunted head, a person who is lame, one without a nose or ears, one with disheveled hair, a one-eyed person, a widow <note>original: "vithali"</note>, or one carrying a staff or weapon comes toward you, it is a sign of danger to one's life. <sig>(187)</sig>

> A woman without breasts, a new pot, one with a rope through their nose,
> A person with a chronic illness, a Garuda bird, or an oil-presser,
> If many of these come before you, the result will be death.

(Meaning) One with disheveled hair, a bald man, a snake-ascetic <note>original: "pambu sanyasi"</note>, a single Brahmin, a woman without breasts, a person carrying a new pot, one with a nose piercing/rope, or a person with a disability—if these come before you, it is inauspicious. <sig>(188)</sig>

### Omens of the Coconut

> If the top is large and the base is small, Lakshmi resides there,
> If broken into three parts, one part brings great joy,
> If the fibers are clean and the water is sweet, wealth follows,
> Those who live in such a house will have long lives,
> But if the break is jagged or the eye is damaged, expect conflict,
> Thus spoke Mayan in the beautiful Tamil tongue,
> Listen to the nature of the omens of the coconut.

(Meaning) When a coconut is broken during an auspicious time <term>Muhurtham: an auspicious moment calculated for rituals</term>, if the top portion is large and the base is small, the Goddess of Wealth <note>Lakshmi</note> will dwell there. If it breaks such that three parts are at the head and one part is at the base, there will be a hundred-fold happiness. If the fibers are clear, they will live a long life. If it looks like mustard <unclear>...</unclear>

If the measurement <note>likely referring to the coconut used in the ritual</note> falls inward, jewels will be acquired. If it opens in the middle, it will cause great sorrow.

Listen to the omens of the coconut as told by Mayan <note>The mythical progenitor of architecture and craftsmanship in Tamil tradition</note>, who is well-versed in the beautiful Tamil language: If it breaks into one or two pieces, it is good. If it breaks into six or four pieces, it brings great suffering. If the "eye" of the coconut is struck and broken, the owner will face death. If the shell pieces scatter, food will become scarce. If the shell is crushed, disease will afflict the body. If it falls to the ground, the owner will lose his property and suffer.

(Meaning): If it breaks exactly in the center, it is beneficial. If it shatters into six or four fragments, it brings much distress. If the eye of the coconut is damaged, the master of the house will die. If the pieces fly apart, there will be no food to eat. If the shell is splintered, bodily illness will occur. If it falls upon the earth, the master will be forced to sell his house and lands and live in grief.

->The Influence of Venus and Jupiter<-

If one begins the construction of great towers, temples, or houses on a Thursday or Friday, with the Sun in the 4th or 7th house of the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of the ritual</note>, and the Moon in the 3rd house, that structure will stand perfectly and flourish for a full hundred years.

<margin>(111)</margin>
(Meaning): When Jupiter <note>Thursday</note> or Venus <note>Friday</note> are the days of the week, and the Sun is in the 4th or 7th house from the Lagna, and the Moon is in the 3rd house—if towers, halls, houses, or palaces are built at such a time, they will endure for a lifespan of one hundred years.

->Auspicious Timing for Laying the Foundation<-

If the Moon stands in the 4th or 10th house from the birth <term>Lagna</term>, and Jupiter, Mars, and Saturn are in the 11th house, a foundation laid at that <term>Muhurtham</term> <note>An auspicious moment or time-slot calculated for beginning a task</note> will grant the house a life of one hundred years. If Jupiter and Venus are in the Lagna, the Sun is in the 3rd, and Mars is in the 6th, the wise say that the house built at that moment will flourish for a century.

<margin>(112)</margin>
(Meaning): If the Moon is in the 4th or 10th house from the birth Lagna, and Mars and Saturn are in the 11th, the house started at that time will last for a hundred years. If Jupiter and Venus are in the Lagna, the Sun is in the 3rd, and Mars is in the 6th, the house built during that Muhurtham will prosper and remain strong for a hundred years.

# Science of House Construction
<note>original: "Manaiyadi Sastram" - a traditional Tamil treatise on architecture and the auspicious timing for building homes.</note>

If Venus is in the tenth house from the rising sign, and Mercury and Jupiter are in the third house while setting, and one performs the auspicious timing for a city or town, the lifespan will likewise be six hundred years.

**Meaning:** If the Moon is in the Lagna, Mercury is in the 4th house, and Jupiter is setting during the ceremony, that house will last for six hundred years. If Venus is in the 10th house and Mercury is in the 3rd house while setting during the ceremony, that house will also flourish for six hundred years.

**(Meaning)** If the ceremony is performed when Mercury is in the house of Venus within Kurukshetra, it will last eight hundred years. If the Moon stands in the tenth house while the Sun is setting and the temple ceremony is performed, it will flourish for a thousand years.

In this method, if the planet Venus is in the residence of Kurukshetra, there will be no news of fire <insert>no danger of fire</insert> and people will live in joy.

**Meaning:** If the ceremony is performed with Venus rising, Jupiter in the 7th house, and the Moon in the 10th house, it will flourish for a thousand years. If Venus is in the Lagna and within Kurukshetra during the ceremony, there is no fear of fire—this is the meaning.

# The Science of Architecture
<note>original: "Shilpa Shastra" - a collection of ancient Indian texts on arts, crafts, and construction laws.</note>

When the wood is tall and the three stars of Uttiram <note>the 12th lunar mansion</note> are present,
Listen to the auspicious timing for the entrance:
When Jupiter resides in the constellation of Mrigashirsha <note>the 5th lunar mansion</note>,
And it is a Thursday, it shall bring the joy of victory.

<margin>(176)</note>
**Meaning:** If the Moon is positioned in Cancer, Capricorn, or Pisces when the foundation pole is erected, victory is assured. And it is a Friday, it is considered an auspicious sight.
If the Moon stands in a water sign
And it is a Monday,
Even if the wife of the house is pregnant,
Or if one performs a wedding ceremony or enters a new house,
The great architect Mayan <note>the divine architect of the asuras in Hindu mythology</note> says
That these actions will be blessed with abundance; look upon this with joy.

<margin>(177)</margin>
**Meaning:** If Venus is in a water sign on a Friday, wealth will be attained. If the Moon is in a water sign on a Monday, even if the wife is pregnant, performing a wedding or entering a house is auspicious. This is the statement spoken by Mayan.

If the Sun is in the house of the entrance, wealth will be without limit.
If the Moon stands there, there will be no labor or noise <note>implying a lack of progress or vitality</note>.
If both are in the foundation with the rising of the fire element,
Those who rule the earth will find great fortune.

## Characteristics for Digging Wells

In Aries, the hidden ground is rocky;
In Taurus, the water is found at a great height;
In Gemini, it is like the seashore;
<margin>(178)</margin>
In Cancer, the water flows in abundance.
In Leo, the King, it is desired but rocky;
In Virgo, it is said that if you dig the stone
Found in the south or the north,
The water will gush out like a roaring spring.
In Libra, there are anthills;
In Scorpio, an ancient well will appear.

<note>A specific meter in Tamil poetry used for technical and moral instructions.</note>

The rising first day and the prominent sixth days,
And the complete eleventh day—these belong to the "Nanda" group;
The result of these, Oh Goddess of Speech, is the loss of wealth. (228)

The unique twelfth day, and the fourteenth lunar day;

Those with knowledge say these will result in auspiciousness. (224)

The great fourth and ninth days,
And the fourteenth day from its beginning;
The result of these "Rikta" <note>Empty or hollow</note> lunar days
Is the fear of death in battle. (230)

The spoken fifth day and the appearing tenth day,
The conquering full moon—these are the "Poorna" <note>Complete</note> lunar days;
The result of embracing these is praised as great victory;
But avoid the dark new moon, Oh Goddess of Speech. (231)

The waxing moon phase is auspicious. After the new moon of the waning phase, from the third day until the seventh day after the full moon, it is considered the waxing moon phase. Other days do not count as the waning phase <insert>for these purposes</insert>. The 1st, 6th, and 11th days are "Nanda" days; their result is loss of wealth. The 2nd, 7th, and 12th are "Bhadra" days; their result is "Bandhanam" (restriction). The 3rd, 8th, and 13th <unclear>...</unclear>

# The Science of Architecture
<note>original: "Shilpa Shastra" - a collection of ancient Indian architectural treatises</note>

This describes the lunar days <term>Tithi: the lunar day in the Hindu calendar</term> and their results. The auspicious days are productive. The ninth and fourteenth lunar days are considered "empty" or "piercing" days <note>original: "Rikta Tithi"</note>; their result is the fear of death. The fifth, tenth, and full moon days are "complete" days <note>original: "Poorna Tithi"</note>; their result is victory. On the new moon day, there is a loss of wealth and a loss of life.

---

### Results of the Weekdays

On the beautiful Sunday and the solitary Tuesday, there is a fear of fire.
The curved lunar days also bring specific results.
The great architect Mayan <note>The divine architect and craftsman in Hindu mythology</note> did not say these were courageous days for building. (232)

Truly, Wednesday and Thursday,
And the coolness of Friday, are auspicious.
Truly, on Saturday, there is a fear of theft.
Understand the nature of the weekdays before building a house. (233)

->Sunday and Tuesday: fear of fire. Monday: neutral. Wednesday, Thursday, and Friday: auspicious. Saturday: fear of theft. One must build a house after knowing these results.<-

### Results of the Stars
<note>original: "Nakshatra" - the 27 lunar mansions or star constellations used in Vedic astrology</note>

The stars Rohini and the ancient Avittam,
The righteous Sadayam,
The faultless Mrigashira and Pushya,
And the righteous Thiruvonam are beneficial. (234)

The beneficial Swati and Anusham,
The faultless Hastam and Chitra,
The abundance of Revati and Uttara Bhadrapada,
And the quality of Uttara Ashadha—these are auspicious. (235)

Observe the star called Uttara Phalguni;
These fourteen stars will grant auspiciousness.
Mayan spoke of these paths,
Having compiled them clearly in this excellent treatise. (236)

Mrigashira, Rohini, Avittam, Sadayam, Thiruvonam, Pushya, Uttara Phalguni, Hastam, Chitra, Swati, Anusham, Uttara Ashadha, Uttara Bhadrapada, and Revati: these fourteen are auspicious.

---

### Results of the Yogas
<note>Yoga: in this context, refers to specific astrological alignments of the sun and moon</note>

The lasting Shubha, Brahma,
The rising Mantra, the praised Saathiya,
The omnipresent Shiva, the cool Shubha, and Brahma,
The beautiful Sukarma and Saubhagya. (237)

# Vastu Shastra <note>The traditional Indian science of architecture and placement</note>

The fixed *Dhruva* and the stable *Vyatipata* are mentioned;
The desired *Shobhana* is sought, while the inauspicious is avoided.
The appearing *Priti* and the visible *Siddha* signs,
These are the fourteen *Yogas* <note>planetary combinations</note> that are declared auspicious. (238)

The harmful *Vyaghata* and the obstructive *Ganda* are spoken of;
The faulty *Shula* and the piercing *Vishkambha* are noted.
The recited *Vajra* and the great obstacle *Atiganda*,
And the lawful but terrible *Vyatipata* are all bad. (239)

The evil *Parigha* is also mentioned;
Along with the well-known *Vaidhrti*, these *Yogas*
Are declared by the master architects <term>Shilpis</term> to be wicked.
These nine combined are praised as being entirely bad. (240)

**Auspicious:** *Prathama, Aindra, Saadhya, Shiva, Shubhra, Saubhagya, Ayushman, Dhriti, Dhruva, Sukarma, Shobhana, Priti, Siddha*—these fourteen are good.

**Inauspicious:** *Vyaghata, Ganda, Atiganda, Vishkambha, Shula, Vajra, Vyatipata, Vaidhrti, Parigha*—these 9 *Yogas* are not acceptable.

->**The Results of the Karanas**<- <note>A Karana is half of a lunar day or Tithi</note>

The performing *Balava* and the strong *Kaulava*,
The noble ones speak of the greatness of *Taitila*.
The calculated *Gara* and the rising *Vanija*,
And the watchful *Bhadra*—these six are auspicious. (241)

The surviving *Shakuni* and the rising *Chatushpada*,
The slowly moving *Naga* and the spoken *Vanija* <note>The text repeats Vanija here, likely referring to the transition to inauspicious phases</note>,
And the truly terrifying *Kinstughna*—these five
Are the false *Karanas* declared to be bad. (242)

**Auspicious:** *Bava, Balava, Kaulava, Taitila, Gara, Bhadra*—these 6 *Karanas* are good.

**Inauspicious:** *Shakuni, Naga, Chatushpada, Kinstughna, Vanija*—these 5 *Karanas* are not acceptable.

->**The Movement of the Sun**<-

The spoken *Bharani* <note>The 2nd Nakshatra/Star</note> in its second quarter,
The flourishing *Krittika* in its fourth quarter,
The desired *Rohini* in its first quarter,
The Sun <term>Paridhi</term> is classified within these seven changes. (243)

When the Sun stands in the *Naadi* <note>a specific energy channel or pulse in the site</note>,
It foretells the cruel effects of blood and fire.
Like the Sun, the architect *Mayan* <note>The mythical progenitor of Vastu Shastra</note> says for humans,
It will result in death and disease; do not ignore this. (244)

## Concerning the Science of Architecture

If one builds a house while the Sun is positioned in the two months of Bharani <note>The second lunar mansion</note>, the four months of Krittika <note>The third lunar mansion</note>, or the first quarter of Rohini—specifically within these seven quarters—there will be a fear of fire.

->The Movement of Mars<-

The months mentioned are the excellent Krittika, the comparable Pushya, Magha, the shining Purva Phalguni, and Hasta; also, the unerring Revati and Mula are considered unfavorable. (245)
O friend, listen to the words of the Guru regarding these unfavorable times: if one builds a house while Mars <note>original: "Angarakan"</note> resides in any of these seven killing asterisms <note>lunar mansions</note>, it will bring harm to one's children. (246)

If a house is built while Mars is in these seven stars—Krittika, Pushya, Magha, Purva Phalguni, Hasta, Mula, and Revati—it results in a deficiency or loss of children <term>Putra-ani: loss or harm to offspring</term>.

->Remedy for these Defects<-

O woman, listen: Pisces, Capricorn, and Cancer are known as the "Full Water Signs." This is the science declared by Brahma <note>original: "Kanja-mayan"</note>, and it does not fail. (247)
Libra, Scorpio, and Aries are classified as "Water-footed Signs." This has been explained clearly by the ancients. (248)
Aquarius, Taurus, and Leo are known as "Half-Water Signs." This is what the great Brahma has graciously spoken. (249)
Virgo, Gemini, and Sagittarius—these three are called "Dry Signs." Among all, the Full Water signs are the most excellent. (250)
If one constructs a beautiful mansion in these auspicious signs, and if Jupiter or Venus are positioned in the best house-signs, (251)
And if the Moon is also favorably placed, then even if a house is built during the aforementioned difficult times, the great defects <term>Dosha: astrological blemish or defect</term> will vanish and lose their power. This is what we have declared according to the established texts. (252)

# Science of House Groundwork
<note>original: "Manaiyadi Sastram" - a traditional architectural treatise</note>

Pisces, Cancer, and Capricorn are full water signs.
Scorpio, Libra, and Aries are partial water signs.
Aquarius, Taurus, and Leo are half water signs.
Virgo, Gemini, and Sagittarius are non-water signs. Even if one builds a house during the full water signs, if it is built under an auspicious star with the gaze of the Moon, Jupiter, or Venus, there is no need to fear the <term>Angaraka Dosha</term> <note>the harmful astrological influence of the planet Mars</note>.

### The Benefits of Mercury

The golden star Hasta, the spoken Ashwini,
The enduring Uttara, and Mrigashira,
The shining Rohini, and the bright Chitra;
These six are grouped together. <margin>(3)</margin>
If Mercury resides in the rising sign,
Or if it is positioned in its highest exaltation,
Building a house with these qualities
Will bring great governance and rule. <margin>(254)</margin>
The rule will bring many children,
Greatness, and an abundance of wealth;
All these shown benefits will bring joy,
This is declared without any doubt. <margin>(255)</margin>

If one begins building a house when Mercury is in any of these six stars—Ashwini, Mrigashira, Hasta, Uttara, Chitra, or Rohini—or if Mercury is in the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the ascending sign at the time of commencement</note> or in its exaltation, they will live happily with the wealth of children.

### The Benefits of Jupiter’s Transit

The auspicious Shravana,
The pure Uttarashada and Uttara Bhadrapada,
The fitting Purvashada, the rising Rohini,
The warm Mrigashira, and the spoken Pushya. <margin>(256)</margin>
The reaching Pushya and the spoken Ashlesha,
Stars that bring fragrance and bravery;
Though some say these nine cause destruction,
They bring strength when approached with love. <margin>(257)</margin>
If Jupiter is full and flourishing in these stars,
Or if it is in the powerful ascending sign or its exaltation,
If the house is built so that wealth may shine,
The Goddess Lakshmi herself will dwell in the center. <margin>(258)</margin>

If one begins building a house when Jupiter is in any of these <term>9 stars</term> <note>original: "9-natchathirathil"</note>—Shravana, Uttarashada, Uttara Bhadrapada, Purvashada, Mrigashira, Ashlesha, Rohini, or Pushya—or if Jupiter is in the ascending sign or its exaltation, the grace of Goddess Lakshmi will be obtained.

# The Science of Architecture

->**The Influence of Venus**<-

If the planet Venus <note>original: "Sukran"</note> appears in the following ten stars: Jyeshtha, Punarvasu, Chitra, Ashwini, Purvashada, Dhanishta, Anuradha, Vishakha, Ardra, and Shatabhisha, it is said to be auspicious. (256-257)

If the aforementioned Venus is positioned strongly in these ten stars, the construction of a house or a mansion will be successful. It will remove all suffering and truly grant happiness to the inhabitants. (258)

Jyeshtha, Punarvasu, Chitra, Ashwini, Purvashada, Dhanishta, Anuradha, Vishakha, Ardra, and Shatabhisha—if Venus resides in these ten stars <term>Nakshatras: lunar mansions in Hindu astrology</term> when building or beginning a house, it will yield auspicious benefits.

## The Effects of Saturn's Transit

If the planet Saturn <note>original: "Sani"</note> resides in these eight stars: Shatabhisha, Purvabhadrapada, Swati, Uttarabhadrapada, Bharani, Dhanishta, Anuradha, and Jyeshtha (262), and if one builds a mansion during this time, the structure will lose its integrity, fall into ruin, and become a desolate place. (263)

Evil spirits <note>original: "Bhuta"</note> will occupy that house; the goddess of misfortune <term>Mudevi: the elder sister of Lakshmi, representing poverty and ill-luck</term> will dwell there, and powerful demons <term>Brahmarakshasas: fierce spirit-beings</term> will always haunt it. The Goddess of Fortune <note>original: "Sridevi"</note> will surely depart from such a place. (264)

Shatabhisha, Purvabhadrapada, Swati, Uttarabhadrapada, Bharani, Dhanishta, Anuradha, and Jyeshtha—if Saturn is in these eight stars when building or starting a house, Brahmarakshasas will take up residence there.

## The Effects of Rahu's Transit

When the shadow planet Rahu <note>original: "Rahu"</note> follows its course and enters the fourteenth star in a retrograde or fast transit <note>original: "atisaram"</note>, it is known as the transit of death. This is considered extremely malevolent and destructive. (265)

If the wicked planets occupy the daytime path,
And one attempts to build a grand mansion there,
Unspeakable suffering will manifest;
Day and night, one will be hidden in misery. (297)
<note>original: "பொல்லாதையர்" (pollathaiyar) - referring to malefic planets like Rahu or Saturn.</note>

If the fourteenth star from the Ascendant <term>Lagnam; the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at a specific time</term> is occupied by Rahu, it is considered a "flag-bearing" path. Therefore, one should not investigate or begin building a house during that transit.

## The Effects of the Setting of Jupiter and Venus

> When the noble Venus or Jupiter
> Reach their point of setting <term>Astamanum; the disappearance of a planet due to its proximity to the Sun</term>,
> Or if they reside in a debilitated sign,
> Slander and enmity will surely arise. (298)

> Truly, if the Lord of the Field <note>The ruling planet of the specific land or zodiac sign.</note>
> Is joined by cold-hearted enemies,
> It is not fit to construct a fertile mansion;
> This is the truth seen and spoken by Brahma himself. (299)

> Regarding the authority over the mansion,
> No matter the status or caste of the lord,
> If the planets governing that specific person
> Are poorly placed, it is an evil omen. (300)

> If the ruling planet is with an enemy,
> Or is shattered by the state of setting,
> Or resides in a house of debilitation,
> Building a mansion then will lead to its ruin. (301)

> To the person marked by such suffering,
> Misfortune will occur at all times;
> Strangers will come together in a crowd,
> And take possession of the house through injustice. (302)

If Jupiter or Venus are set (combust), or in a state of debilitation, or in an enemy field, or joined with enemy planets, it is not auspicious. Regardless of the owner's background, if the planets corresponding to their nature are joined with enemies, set, or debilitated, then building a house will result in others seizing the property.

## The Effects of Fixed Ascendant Signs

> The youthful Lion (Leo), the spoken Bull (Taurus),
> The earthly Water-Bearer (Aquarius), and the Scorpio;
> These beautiful and significant fields,
> Are traditionally good and excellent for building. (303)
<note>These are the four "Fixed" signs of the zodiac, believed to provide stability to a structure.</note>

# The Science of Architecture

The signs of Cancer, Aries,
Libra, and the distinguished Capricorn—
These four are known as the Moveable Signs <note>Sanskrit: *Chara Lagna*. In Vedic astrology, these signs represent change and movement.</note>.
Observe that these are considered mediocre for building according to this architectural treatise. (273)

The elegant Virgo, the beautiful Gemini,
The pure Pisces, and the following Sagittarius—
These four are known as the Dual Signs <note>Sanskrit: *Ubhaya Rashi*. These signs possess a nature that is both fixed and moveable.</note>.
They are considered auspicious in nature, yet they are complex. (274)

If one builds a house during these times,
The results will be as harsh as a dense forest; it is not good.
If the Lord of the Ascendant <note>The ruling planet of the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of commencement.</note> is weakened
Or positioned in an unfavorable house, it is said to be ill-fated. (275)

If the Ascendant falls in an enemy's abode,
Or if the Lord of that Ascendant
Resides in a house belonging to a foe,
Or if it occupies the tenth house from the sign of Cancer— (276)

If a beautiful house is constructed during such a time,
It will not bring the expected prosperity.
Like a bird of prey circling, the property will fall into the hands of others.
This was clearly explained by the divine architect, Mayan. (277)

Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, and Aquarius: these four are Fixed Signs <note>Sanskrit: *Sthira Lagna*</note> and are the most excellent for building. Aries, Cancer, Libra, and Capricorn: these four are Moveable Signs and are mediocre. Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, and Pisces: because these four are Dual Signs, one should not begin building a house during their influence. If the Lord of the Ascendant is in a house hostile to that planet, or if the Lord is in the tenth house from the starting Ascendant, building at such a time will cause the property to be alienated <note>The text uses "Paratheenam," meaning the property will pass into the control or ownership of strangers or enemies.</note>.

## Signs Unsuitable for Specific Directions

For the established East, the "Flag" sign <note>Original: *Dhuvajayam*. This refers to a specific calculation in the *Ayadi* varga used to determine the lifespan and energy of a building.</note>
Is unsuitable if it falls under the sign of Scorpio.
For the South-East, which is associated with smoke,
The sign of Pisces is likewise unsuitable. (278)

For the South, which is associated with the Lion,
And the South-West, which is associated with the Dog,
And the West, which is associated with the Ox—
The sign of Sagittarius is unsuitable for these. (279)

For the North-West, which is associated with the Deer,
The sign of Libra is unsuitable.
In such a place, a person of high status should not reside.
This was precisely declared by the sage Mayan. (280)

# The Science of House-Building <note>original: "Manaiyadi Sastram" - a traditional Tamil manual for architecture and Vastu</note>

<unclear>Faded text regarding northern placement</unclear>
I place the Elephant orientation <note>Gaja-ayam</note> in the North;
This orientation is not suitable for those born under the sign of Aries <note>Mesha rasi</note>.
This was spoken by the sage Annamayanar for all to know. (281)

The Crow orientation <note>Kaka-ayam</note> shines in the North-East;
It is not suitable for those born under the sign of Aquarius <note>Kumbha rasi</note>.
Capricorn, Gemini, Leo, and Taurus—
These signs are also mentioned in this calculation. (282)

For those born under the aforementioned signs,
If they build a house or a terraced mansion in these spots,
It will not bring victory or prosperity.
This was declared in the Tamil songs of the ancient sages. (283)

If one builds a mansion or palace against these rules,
The flaws <note>dosham</note> will result in a house of sorrow.
The lineage will not flourish,
And the structure will quickly fall into decay and ruin. (284)

---

The Flag orientation <note>Dhvaja-ayam</note> in the **East** is not for those of the **Scorpio** sign.
The Smoke orientation <note>Dhuma-ayam</note> in the **South-East** is not for those of the **Pisces** sign.
The Lion orientation <note>Simha-ayam</note> in the **South** is not for those of the **Cancer** sign.
The Dog orientation <note>Sunaka-ayam</note> in the **South-West** is not for those of the **Virgo** sign.
The Bull orientation <note>Rishaba-ayam</note> in the **West** is not for those of the **Sagittarius** sign.
The Donkey orientation <note>Khara-ayam</note> in the **North-West** is not for those of the **Libra** sign.
The Elephant orientation <note>Gaja-ayam</note> in the **North** is not for those of the **Aries** sign.
The Crow orientation <note>Kaka-ayam</note> in the **North-East** is not for those of the **Aquarius** sign.
Those belonging to **Capricorn, Gemini, Leo, and Taurus** should not build houses in the center of the village.

If a house is built according to the light of these rules, it will avoid becoming a void <note>suniyam - a place of spiritual or material emptiness</note>.
**A, Ā, I, Ī, U, Ū, E, Ē** <note>The first eight vowels of the Tamil alphabet, used here to categorize directions and symbolic animals</note>
These correspond to the East-facing directions; their results are as follows:

1. Indra (East): The **Garuda** <note>Mythical eagle</note> — (Syllables: Ka, Kā, Ki, Kī, Ku)
2. Agni (SE): The **Cat** — (Syllables: Ca, Cā, Ci, Cī, Cu)
3. Yama (South): The **Lion** — (Syllables: Ta, Tā, Ti, Tī, Tu)
4. Nirriti (SW): The **Dog** — (Syllables: Tha, Thā, Thi, Thī, Thu)
5. Varuna (West): The **Snake** — (Syllables: Pa, Pā, Pi, Pī, Pu)
6. Vayu (NW): The **Rat** — (Syllables: Ya, Ra, La, Va)
7. Kubera (North): The **Elephant** — (Syllables: Sa, Sha, Sa, Ha)
8. Isanya (NE): The **Rabbit** — (Syllables: Ka, Ta, Na...)

Because the fifth position from a starting point represents the "Place of the Enemy" <note>Sathru-sthanam</note>, one must not build a house there. If built, it will become a void. One must build a house only after understanding these enmities.

# Determining Village Sites and Unfavorable Land Shapes <unclear>Comparison</unclear>

<margin>(285)</margin>
Like a pair of scissors <note>original: "kattiri"; referring to a cross-shape or X-shape</note> appearing,
Or like the three corners <note>a triangle</note> described,
Or like the straight angle <note>possibly a sharp linear or skewed alignment</note> mentioned,
Or even like the shape of a human head.

<margin>(286)</margin>
Like a drum <note>original: "mattalam"; a barrel shape</note> or like an areca nut,
With an interior and exterior that are uneven,
In this beautiful world,
Such lands are not fit for a ruler to inhabit.

<margin>(287)</margin>
In a village where saltiness increases,
Or in a corner where the chief resides,
Even if it is a sought-after low-lying land,
If it is land where water constantly runs off <note>referring to erosion or poor drainage</note>.

<margin>(288)</margin>
Where many people debate the income,
In land where stability cannot be achieved,
In land belonging to a temple tower <note>original: "gopuram"</note>,
Or where there are anthills inhabited by hooded serpents.

<margin>(289)</margin>
Along with anthills, land that is small and husked <note>infertile</note>,
Even if it is a square plot of land,
If it is situated near a cremation ground,
Or a cattle shed where animals low and cry out.

<margin>(290)</margin>
Even if the house is built with justice,
Facing the great West or the East,
If there is water or the fame of fire nearby,
It is like ghee <note>clarified butter</note> shining while the Vedas are chanted.

<margin>(291)</margin>
Ghee, curd, and fresh honey,
Plucked oil, and the sign of blood,
Fear, along with a corpse,
And a hot cattle pen—these are all evil.

<margin>(292)</margin>
These methods of entry are evil;
The experts of the <term>Shilpa Shastra</term> <note>traditional science of sculpture and architecture</note> who have clearly understood
And seen the strength of these truths,
Know that this is the true vision of the great texts.

->**Prose Summary of the Verses**<-

Land shaped like scissors, or with three corners, or like an eye, or like a head—or shaped like a drum, or like an areca nut, or land that is uneven between its interior and exterior; a village with salty soil, or land in a corner, or low-lying land, or land where water runs off; land that many deem unstable, or temple land, or land with anthills, or land near a cremation ground—or land where sheep and cattle are tied; land where the width between East and West is insufficient, or land too close to water...

# The Treatise of Maya
<note>original: "Mayanul Sastram" — A classical Tamil text on Vastu Shastra (architecture) attributed to the divine architect Maya.</note>

<warning>The top section of the page is heavily stained and faded, making several words in the opening paragraph difficult to decipher.</warning>

<unclear>Honey, ghee, sugar, blood, fish, corpses, birds—in places</unclear>
<unclear>possessing the scent of these things,</unclear> one should not build a house. If built, various miseries will arise.

## Rules for Testing the Earth and Building Houses

The earth that is marked by a white hue,
With a desirable taste of sweetness,
And the fitting fragrance of the great lotus—
If the earth is thus, it is very good to stay there. (292)

The masters of the good texts say of this mansion:
The family shall never diminish in its days,
Prosperity will rise well, and like a <term>Karpagam</term><note>The celestial wish-fulfilling tree</note>,
The residence shall be classified as a strong, divine abode. (294)

The earth spoken of as a high red color,
The earth considered to have an astringent taste,
And possessing the beautiful scent of a horse—
On such land, build a mansion of fame. (295)

The mansion built thus for the kings of the earth,
As told by Kubera <note>The god of wealth</note> who dwells in the city,
Will be free from suffering, possess great beauty,
And they shall live in preciousness without any obstacles. (296)

White earth—sweet earth, earth with the fragrance of the lotus flower: in these, it is excellent for <term>Brahmins</term><note>The priestly/scholarly class</note> to build houses.

Red earth, astringent earth—earth with the scent of horses: in these, it is excellent for <term>Kings</term> to build houses.

Black earth—bitter or pungent—earth with the scent of grains: in these, it is excellent for <term>Sudras</term><note>The laboring/artisan class</note> to build houses.

Green earth, possessing sour or salty tastes: in these, it is excellent for the <term>Sangama</term> communities to build houses.

In land that possesses both sweetness and fragrance, it is excellent for all classes to build houses.

---

## Divination of the Field
<note>original: "Kshetra Arudam" — A method of predicting the suitability of a plot of land using numerology or astrology.</note>

When asking if it is good to build a house on a particular date, take the count of the <unclear>Nanda</unclear> date for the person and place it on the right side. Take the count for the letters of the person's name and place it on the left side. Divide the total by 8; the remainder is "Wealth." Take the count from the right side and place it on the left, divide by 8; the remainder is "Quality" <note>or Character of the house</note>. If Wealth is high and Quality is low, it is [acceptable]; if Quality is high and Wealth is low, it is not good.

### The Position of the Kaula Wheel
<note>The Kaula Sakkaram is an astrological diagram used to determine the compatibility between the house owner's zodiac sign and the building site.</note>
Starting from the Northeast <note>original: "Eesaniyam"</note>, place the length and write the 12 zodiac signs <note>original: "Rasis"</note> in a clockwise direction <note>original: "Pradakshinam"</note> around this wheel. Count from the position assigned to the patron's zodiac sign and speak the results. Since this is a wheel, if one builds a house in the patron's own sign or the fifth sign from it, the house will nourish his relatives. If one builds a house in the 7th, 11th, or 8th signs from his own, it will nourish strangers. If one builds in the 2nd, 4th, or 7th signs, the results will be neutral.

| Pisces | Aries | Taurus | Gemini |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Aquarius** | | **The Kaula Wheel** | **Cancer** |
| **Capricorn** | | **Observe starting from Pisces** | **Leo** |
| **Sagittarius** | **Scorpio** | **Libra** | **Virgo** |

<note>The table above is a representation of the zodiac wheel (Kaula Sakkaram) used in architectural astrology. The 12 signs are arranged around a central space containing the title and instructions to begin calculations from Pisces (Meenam).</note>

### (Characteristics of testing the soil by digging a pit)
He who intends to build a house should measure a pit one cubit <note>term: "Muzham" - a traditional unit of measurement approximately the length of a forearm from the elbow to the tip of the middle finger</note> in width, length, and depth. Ensure all four sides and the bottom depth are equal. Dig the pit and then fill it back in with the same soil. If there is excess soil remaining, it is auspicious <note>indicating "living" earth that expands</note>. If the soil fits exactly, it is neutral. If the soil is insufficient to fill the pit, it indicates poverty <note>original: "Daridriyam"</note>. Furthermore, when the sun has set, remove the soil from the aforementioned pit and fill it to the brim with water. Upon checking at sunrise the next day, if water or mud remains, it is auspicious. If there is no water or mud in that pit, or if insects are seen running within it, it is not good. When water and milk are poured into the said pit, if they swirl in a clockwise direction, it is a good omen.

For the land of Brahmins and Kshatriyas <note>The priestly and warrior/ruling classes</note>, one must first touch the ground with the knee. Then, the Brahmin should touch the ground with Darbha grass <note>term: "Darbha" - a sacred grass used in Vedic rituals</note>...

...should be measured. For a Vaishya <note>The merchant or land-owning class</note>, it should be measured with a staff of *Tridanda* <note>original: "திரிதருத்தண்டால்"; likely referring to a sacred triple-staff</note>. For Kshatriyas <note>The warrior or ruling class</note>, it should be measured with a rope made of teak fiber. For Vaishyas, it should be measured with a rope made of palmyra fiber. For Shudras <note>The laboring or farming class</note>, it should be measured with a three-ply cotton thread. With this specific rope, one may measure for temples, Brahmin houses, and Kshatriya houses. Subsequently, a two-ply cotton rope may be used to measure for the houses of Vaishyas and Shudras.

When holding this rope to measure, whichever living creature crosses over it, the bones of that particular species will be found beneath the soil. Those must be removed. While measuring with the aforementioned rope:
* If a **chicken** crosses, bird bones will be found in the East corner.
* If a **cat** crosses, its bones will be found in the *Agni* corner <note>South-East</note>.
* If an **eagle** <note>or kite</note> crosses, rabbit bones will be found in the *Nairuthi* corner <note>South-West</note>.
* If a **cow** crosses, its bones will be found in the *Varuna* corner <note>West</note>.
* If a **donkey** crosses, its bones will be found in the *Vayu* corner <note>North-West</note>.
* If a **horse** crosses, its bones will be found in the North corner.
* If a **human** crosses, human bones will be found in the center.

If a man who has smeared himself with oil stands and looks toward a certain corner, if one digs to a depth of 11 inches <note>original: "எக" (11) inches</note> in that direction, human bones will be found. If a person who is mentally ill <note>literally "possessed by madness"</note> looks toward a certain corner, if one digs 8 inches <note>original: "அ" (8) inches</note> in that corner, human bones will be found. In this manner, one must test the earth before building a house.

->**Characteristics of Bones Remaining in the House Site**<-

The consequences of building a house without testing the earth are as follows:
* If there are cows but they yield no milk, the bones of a **child** are in the East.
* If the cows yield no milk and there is constant anger and obstruction, **horse** bones are in the *Agni* corner <note>South-East</note>; furthermore, the resident will perpetually suffer from chronic sores or wounds.
* If **pig** bones are in the *Niruthi* corner <note>South-West</note>, the defect of untimely death will occur.
* If **ox** bones are in the West, there will always be disputes and rumors regarding one's movements.
* If **donkey** bones are present, there will be a constant loss of wealth.
* If **goat** bones are in the North, there will be constant quarrels with neighbors.
* If **dog** bones are in the *Ishanya* <note>North-East</note>, there will be perpetual discord in the house.
* If a **skull** <note>original: "தமண்டை" (skull/cranium)</note> is in the center of the house, one must remove the soil to a depth of six spans <note>term: <term>Span (Chaan)</term>: A traditional unit of length measured from the tip of the thumb to the tip of the little finger</note>, test it, and then build to ensure happiness.

If temples are to be built, one must remove soil to a depth of seven spans across the entire extent of that land, test it, and then build. For a Vaishya, 4 spans <note>original: "ச" (4)</note> of soil should be removed and tested. For a Shudra, 3 spans <note>original: "௩" (3)</note> of soil should be removed and tested.

Water should not be allowed to stagnate or flow haphazardly from one's property. If the water from a house flows toward the East, there will be profit. If it flows toward the Southeast <note>original: "Agni-moolai" or the corner of the Fire God</note>, it brings destruction. If it flows toward the South, it brings loss. If it flows toward the Northwest <note>original: "Vayu-moolai" or the corner of the Wind God</note>, it will cause enmity with one's kinsmen. If it flows toward the North, wealth will be created. If it flows toward the Northeast <note>original: "Ishanya," the corner of Lord Shiva</note>, all blessings will be attained. If water stagnates within the house itself, it leads to poverty.

->**The Characteristics and Signs of the Foundation Pit**<-

On a flawless plot of land, dig a square pit
One cubit <note>a traditional unit of length based on the forearm</note> in depth and width;
Fill it with water and place a flower within;
If it circles toward the sun, moving to the right, the dwellers will prosper;
But if it circles to the left, building a house there brings only evil.

(Meaning): On the chosen ground, dig a square pit measuring one cubit in both depth and width. Fill it with water. Take a flower in your hand, meditate upon Lord Shiva, and place it in the water. If the flower rotates clockwise <note>original: "valam," following the path of the sun</note>, the residents will enjoy great prosperity, wealth, fame, royal favor, and an abundance of vehicles; it is a sign of auspiciousness. However, if that flower rotates counter-clockwise <note>to the left</note>, the elders say that building a house there will not only cause great suffering but will also lead to constant danger and the loss of one's wife.
